# Motion scan baseline

transitions-agent 0.11.2: **33/100**, 283 files.

This scans repository sources, design-system examples, and historical backups. It is not a live Framer visual audit. The scanner recognizes only two design-system modal selectors, not the five proposed Index enhancements.

## Recognized components

- `code/design-system/css/components.css:1235` `.mh-lightbox`: off the motion scale; open/close 260ms; [modal recipe](https://transitions.dev/transitions/modal-open-close) expects 250/150ms.
- `code/design-system/css/components.css:1257` `.mh-lightbox__media`: off the motion scale; open/close 260ms; [modal recipe](https://transitions.dev/transitions/modal-open-close) expects 250/150ms.

## off-scale (2)

- `code/design-system/css/components.css:1246` — modal close: 260ms -> 150ms (var(--duration-quick)). Closes should get out of the way, quicker than opens
- `code/design-system/css/components.css:1262` — modal close: 260ms -> 150ms (var(--duration-quick)). Closes should get out of the way, quicker than opens

## slow-duration (5)

- `code/design-system/css/components.css:305` — Transition runs 2s via --mh-duration-draw (defined at code/design-system/tokens/tokens.css:177). UI transitions should stay well under a second.
- `code/design-system/tokens/tokens.css:177` — Motion token --mh-duration-draw is 2s - every transition using it drags. Bring it onto the duration scale (150-400ms).
- `code/design-system/css/components.css:1310` — Transition runs 1.2s via --mh-duration-page (defined at code/design-system/tokens/tokens.css:176). UI transitions should stay well under a second.
- `code/design-system/tokens/tokens.css:176` — Motion token --mh-duration-page is 1.2s - every transition using it drags. Bring it onto the duration scale (150-400ms).

## inconsistent-durations (1)

- `(project):0` — 19 different animation durations in use (140ms, 200ms, 260ms, 300ms, 340ms, 360ms, 420ms, 450ms, ...). Consolidate to 2 or 3 motion tokens.
